# CosmicPVE Development Handoff

Current accepted baseline: Pitch Perfect + Anti Gank + Pacify — IMPLEMENTED, AUTOMATED/RUNTIME VERIFIED, MANUALLY VERIFIED/ACCEPTED, and included in this selective closure commit. The preceding Cinderwolf milestone is `ecb88972460c9556c55081c4e14f1b0344f231cf`. User design/source-art changes and later unaccepted Cinderwolf/Space Chest follow-ups are excluded from this closure.

Repository state: Steps 6A–8N and the earlier Conquest synchronization are implemented, verified, and manually accepted. The user-maintained `docs/Cosmic_Design.md` and root `assets`, `blockbench`, `drafts`, and `woodlands` development-source folders remain outside closure ownership.

The user explicitly confirmed “Belts finally work.” The Belt/Omni candidate is accepted for selective closure; user-maintained source assets remain excluded.

Last handoff update: 2026-09-27

## Pitch Perfect + Anti Gank + Pacify accepted (2026-09-27)

Follow-up Trial usability hotfix: Pitch Perfect Harp playback now targets each active participant at the listener position through the player sound channel at 4.0 volume, removing distance attenuation from the plate/button locations. Cinderwolf defeat presentation retains its bold orange wording but splits it into the shorter title `Cinderwolf down!` and subtitle `Find the exit portal!` so the full message fits at ordinary GUI scales. Trial death handling now reconciles dead participants before room ticking and again on death-respawn if the death event did not remove them; an inactive respawn also receives fresh HUD-clear packets. A solo death therefore closes the Trial, preventing `/cosmic trial debug complete-room` from advancing that dead player's room. Loaded Inventor solo-death/direct-event/respawn-fallback regression passes with all 31 required GameTests. Human confirmation of actual HUD clearing and title fit is still needed.

Status: IMPLEMENTED: YES; AUTOMATED/RUNTIME VERIFIED: JUnit `cleanTest test build` 754/754 in 183 suites and 31/31 required loaded GameTests, including the authored Pitch Perfect structure and Inventor death cleanup; dedicated-server startup reached `Done (0.649s)`; client reload included `mod/cosmicpve`, SoundEngine startup and item/block atlas creation without new missing-model errors (installed Slightly Improved Font metadata error is unrelated); MANUALLY VERIFIED/ACCEPTED: YES by the user's explicit next-milestone instruction; COMMITTED: YES in this selective closure commit. User-maintained `docs/Cosmic_Design.md`, root `assets/`, `blockbench/`, and `woodlands/` remain outside closure ownership. Do not begin Abandoned Spaceship.

- Native Apprentice room `cosmicpve:trial/pitch_perfect` consumes the supplied `trial rooms/pitch_perfect.nbt`, published as `cosmicpve:trial/pitch_perfect` by the existing structure pipeline. It joins Cold Snap, Circuit Circus, Raiding Rainbow and Zero-G under the shared 5→3→1→ineligible weight and previous-room exclusion. The 17×10×21 authored room has Emerald spawn marker `[8,1,10]`, normally replaced with Blackstone; four light-weighted center plates at `[8,2,9]`, `[7,2,10]`, `[9,2,10]`, `[8,2,11]`; and left/right portal water columns at x 1–2 / 14–15, z 2–4 / 9–11 / 16–18, y 1–5. Each portal's two bamboo replay buttons are at x 2 or 14, y 3, and z pairs `(1,5)`, `(8,12)`, `(15,19)`. Loaded GameTest validates actual placement/markers/controls, initialization, cleanup and reuse.
- The party size is snapshotted at room entry; 1/2/3/4 participants require 3/4/5/6 successful rounds. Each server-owned round partially shuffles 0–24 and selects six distinct integer note IDs without replacement, assigns them one-to-one to portals, then uniformly chooses one candidate as target. Playback is vanilla Note Block Harp using the canonical note-ID-to-pitch formula. Four plates replay the target; either of two buttons replays its portal's assigned note without changing state. Only active party players entering a water trigger can guess. Correct answers add exactly one success and send bold italic `#E1BAE8` `Pitch number N identified! Only X more to go!`; nonfinal success returns the party to the Emerald spawn and rerolls. Wrong answers preserve completed/required counts but return the entire party and reroll the current numbered round. Final correct guess immediately invokes shared room completion/Decision Box. An explicit round generation, resolving flag, and same-tick guard reject stale/simultaneous resolution; transition/failure/abort cleanup drops the entire attempt state. There is no invented loadout.
- Anti Gank is ordinary Elite Axe I–IV, random-pool eligible but not curated Cosmic Table/V-Kit. For a qualifying ordinary outgoing melee/projectile calculation, it scans living non-allies within 10 blocks **of the attacker**, including the current target if present, through the canonical general ally resolver. Its additive ordinary outgoing bonus is `min(enemyCount, 3×level)%`: I–IV caps 3/6/9/12%. It is dynamic per damage event, with no proc, cooldown, Luck interaction or stack. The existing root outgoing-contributor phase applies it once; snapshot-derived child packets bypass that phase. Nonordinary/true and DOT/environmental damage are excluded.
- Pacify is ordinary Ultimate **Bow-only** I–IV, random-pool eligible but not curated Cosmic Table/V-Kit. A committed damaging parent Bow projectile hit uses the firing-weapon snapshot and normal Luck-relative ProcEngine for base 2/4/6/8% chances; melee, Crossbow, miss, rejected/zero-damage and child packets fail the condition. On success, the living non-ally target receives a generalized `cosmicpve:pacify` NEGATIVE CombatStack of maximum 1, 60 ticks, refresh-all. Generalized stack potency preserves the successful enchantment level; a later successful application refreshes to 60 ticks and replaces potency/source rather than combining levels. While active it contributes −1/−2/−3/−4% only to its holder's ordinary outgoing damage (including hostile mobs). Normal stack expiry, cleansing and `/cosmic stack list <target>` apply; stack inspection now includes nonzero potency. Regular books, `/enchanter`, Dust, Tinkerer, Black Scroll, Transmog, capacity and suppression follow `CosmicEnchantmentSpecs.ALL` without special pools.
- Focused JUnit covers the Apprentice pool/weight, fixed round count, 100 deterministic six-unique-note generations, authored NBT marker/plate/button/water coordinates, target/replay maps, correct/wrong/final/race transitions and exact message styling; enchantment metadata/chances/caps; and one-stack Pacify source/level replacement and exact expiry. Global exact-count snapshots were updated to 85 enchantments and 16 Trial room definitions. All 754 JUnit tests pass. All 31 loaded GameTests pass, including Pitch Perfect authored structure placement/cleanup/reuse and Inventor solo-death session cleanup.
- `git diff --check` on Phase B-owned files passes. The whole-tree invocation reports trailing whitespace only in the pre-existing, user-maintained `docs/Cosmic_Design.md` diff; that file was deliberately neither edited nor staged by this task.
- Audited manual commands: `/cosmic trial portal give @s`, enter the Trial, then `/cosmic trial debug force-room pitch_perfect`; `/cosmic trial debug status` and `/cosmic trial debug abort` are the existing control/cleanup surfaces. In one-player mode, verify three successful rounds; step on all four center plates and compare target Harp note, press each of two buttons per portal and compare their Harp notes, enter a correct water column, verify the exact colored progress message and Emerald return, intentionally enter a wrong column on a later round and verify no progress loss/extra round but full party return and fresh note mapping/target, then finish and verify immediate Decision Box. Repeat with 2–4 players to check 4–6 fixed rounds and near-simultaneous guesses. On an Axe/Bow, use `/cosmic enchant book give @s cosmicpve:anti_gank 1 100 100` (repeat levels 2–4) and `/cosmic enchant book give @s cosmicpve:pacify 1 100 100` (repeat levels 2–4); apply via ordinary inventory UI. Check Anti Gank's current target, 0/1/multiple nearby enemies, 10-block boundary, party/owned ally exclusion and per-level cap. For Pacify, shoot hostile mobs and, where permitted, enemy players; inspect `/cosmic stack list <target>`, compare outgoing damage over 60 ticks, reproc at a different level to verify refreshed duration/replaced potency, and confirm Crossbow cannot accept/proc Pacify. These human gameplay steps remain **unverified** and are not claimed as automated observations.

## Death Coffin + Pyre + Cinder Wolf + Cinderwolf Belt candidate (2026-09-22)

Status: IMPLEMENTED: YES; AUTOMATED/RUNTIME VERIFIED: YES for the accepted candidate (29/29 GameTests pass; focused Cinderwolf JUnit passes; the previous full 747-test JUnit run passed before the new untracked `trial rooms/pitch_perfect.nbt` introduced an unrelated structure-inventory expectation failure; isolated dedicated-server `Done (7.689s)` and client resource/sound/item-atlas startup); MANUALLY VERIFIED/ACCEPTED: YES by the user's next-milestone instruction, with the separately reported instant Pup deaths retained as a known bug; COMMITTED: YES as `ecb88972460c9556c55081c4e14f1b0344f231cf`. Pushed to `origin/master` at `https://github.com/ShouldBeDoingHW/CosmicPVE.git`; `git ls-remote origin refs/heads/master` independently verified the same full hash. The final accepted Pup interval is 240 ticks/12 seconds, superseding the stale 15-second statement in the next-milestone brief. Closed predecessor commit: `9b28c9f5173737cd5935bb7ec08dd55e62bde47b`. Do not begin Abandoned Spaceship or edit the live Google Drive Design Doc.

- Ordinary Death Coffin is Legendary I–III on the all-weapons tag (Sword/Axe/Bow/Crossbow), random-pool eligible but not Cosmic Table curated. A committed ordinary parent hit qualifies only when the original non-ally target was at strictly under 33% max red health at hit entry (Execute-style pre-hit threshold). The finalized ordinary parent-damage snapshot is delivered once to every *other* living non-ally within level 1/2/3 blocks through linked ordinary child delivery with `NO_PROCS`; vanilla armor/Protection and permitted defense run per secondary target. The attacker, original target and canonical allies/owned allies are excluded. Projectile events use the firing-weapon snapshot, not the current hand. Separate true/bleed/lightning child packets cannot be copied or recursively proc Death Coffin.
- Ordinary Pyre is Simple Axe I–III, 5/10/15% base chance via the normal Luck-relative `ProcEngine`, valid committed melee Axe parent only, setting 60 fire ticks without an immediate extra damage packet. Both new specs join ordinary Unexamined/Enchanter/Dust/Tinkerer/Black Scroll/Transmog/book/capacity paths through `CosmicEnchantmentSpecs.ALL`; neither joins the curated Cosmic Table or V-Kit pools.
- `Belt: Cinderwolf` is bold `#852C14`, attached through the existing Belt/Omni socket and leggings loadout. The ordinary incoming pipeline multiplies only sources in the vanilla fire damage tag (including in-fire/lava) by `0.85`; true fireball damage, fall/explosion/nonfire damage remain untouched. `CinderwolfProcFactor` centrally changes the Luck factor from `L` to `L+0.15` for exactly Pyre, Molten and Divine Immolation; other chance multipliers remain independent. Pyre III with no other Luck is `0.15×1.15 = 17.25%`, not 30%. It is room-exclusive in production reward catalogs; admin `/cosmic accessory give cinderwolf @s` remains a test fixture. There is no authored Belt art, so its item uses the existing vanilla-leather icon fallback and has no new worn cosmetic layer; the provided Cinder Wolf art is the mob texture.
- Native Hardcore room ID `cosmicpve:trial/cinder_wolf` consumes the *existing* user-authored `trial rooms/cinderwolf.nbt`, packaged byte-for-byte as `cosmicpve:trial/cinderwolf`. The authored 33×20×34 structure has Emerald [14,0,1] party spawn, stripped cherry wood [27,0,25] boss marker replaced by Nether Bricks on successful spawn, and Diamond [1,6,32] exit marker. Normal 5→3→1→ineligible selection, previous-room exclusion and later-phase eligibility apply. User source was preserved. Mob texture copied to `src/main/resources/assets/cosmicpve/textures/entity/cinder_wolf.png` (64×32); boss/Pup use Wolf renderers scaled 2.25/0.75 visually. Follow-up hotfix: the boss collision box now matches its 2.25× visual scale (1.35×1.9125 blocks), while Pup collision remains vanilla-sized.
- Every entrant's temporary loadout: Iron Helmet/Chestplate/Leggings/Boots all Protection II + Unbreaking III. Helmet has Ender Shift III + Planetary Deathbringer III and real Heroic armor state; chest has Godly Overload III + Aegis VI and real Heroic state; legs Nutrition III + Cactus II; boots Gears III. Hotbar 1: Diamond Axe Sharpness V, Unbreaking III, Death Coffin III, Hex V, Insanity VIII, Pummel III. Slots 2–7: one Splash Water Bottle each. Slot 8: 5 Golden Apples. Slot 9: 32 Baked Potatoes. The existing Trial inventory transaction clears them on room transition/exit.
- Boss snapshots `200+150×(participants−1)` HP (200/350/500/650), armor 2.5, toughness 1, speed .35, melee 7.5. Boss and Pups use fire-immune registered entity types, not reactive healing. Starting after 160 active ticks, each 160 ticks four room-owned SmallFireballs travel N/E/S/W. Impact cancels vanilla fireball effects and emits a terrain-safe visual/sound plus one radius-2.5 player-only packet of 2 HP true damage (armor/absorption/custom-reduction bypass), then sets 100 fire ticks; later ordinary burning may be mitigated by the Belt. Starting after 240 active ticks (12 seconds), every 240 ticks `2+participants` Pups spawn; each is a 0.75-scale Wolf with 5 HP, armor 0, melee 6, fire immunity, and participant targeting. Waves accumulate. Boss death stops future waves/volleys, creates the Diamond-marker gateway exactly once, and displays the bold orange title `Cinderwolf down! Find the exit portal!` to room participants; *entering* the gateway completes the room. Trial cleanup discards boss, Pups and active fireballs and suppresses their drops/XP.
- Physical Cinder Wolf gateway completion alone sets the room-specific reward eligibility flag. Its `Cinderwolf Belt` weight-5 quantity-1 row is appended only to that one active-phase (Hardcore/Impossible/Demonic) weighted table selection; selecting it occupies the normal single reward position. No global table mutation, extra independent roll or generic skip reward. `/cosmic trial debug complete-room` does not set this flag. Force-room now preserves a current higher phase, allowing later-phase manual payout checks.
- Focused JUnit covers metadata, threshold/radii, Pyre chances/duration, additive Luck math and eligible IDs, exact scaling/timers/cardinals/true packet, source resources and ephemeral reward table. Loaded GameTests exercise the authored marker structure, boss stats/fire immunity, tick-160 four-fireball volley, tick-240/480 Pup waves, exit/cleanup, full registry-backed loadout, ordinary Death Coffin child damage, fire/lava-only Belt mitigation and real centralized ProcEngine 11.5%/13.5% checks. No human kill-time/volley/wave/pressure observation has occurred; **do not rebalance** before user feedback.
- Follow-up hotfix: the Decision Box's 30-second expiry closes any still-open Deal/No Deal menu before advancing; it does not close an unrelated menu. A loaded GameTest covers the server-side menu close. The boss hitbox is now 1.35×1.9125 blocks, matching its 2.25× Wolf render scale; its loaded encounter test asserts the dimensions. Post-hotfix `test` and all 28 GameTests pass. The earlier dedicated-server/client startup checks predate only this bounded follow-up.
- Gentle Breeze follow-up: only non-shifting players in active rooms other than Cold Snap and Deadeye receive the pull. Data-driven acceleration was halved again from `0.004` to `0.002` per tick, and the runtime caps older persisted selections at `0.002`; the `0.15` maximum horizontal speed cap is unchanged. The description and focused regression tests reflect these exemptions.
- Cinder Wolf fire/AI follow-up: the authored room's 1,695 stripped mangrove wood blocks are replaced at initialization with a custom nonflammable pillar block that renders the same vanilla mangrove model in all three axes; the 142 authored fire blocks remain. Pup waves search for air above a sturdy floor and avoid fire, walls and entity collisions. Boss/Pups use participant-only targeting instead of tame-wolf goals, then pursue and attempt melee strikes at 20-tick intervals. A loaded GameTest verifies the complete wood conversion, burn odds zero, safe Pup positions, and repeated Pup melee attempts against a participant. Minecraft's mock players reject damage until their client connection finishes loading, so damage acceptance requires manual in-game verification. Visual and balance acceptance remain pending.
- Known bug (user-reported, not investigated in this patch): Cinderwolf Pups still die instantly in-game despite spawning on the floor. Do not treat the existing safe-spawn and aggro GameTests as proof that this is resolved.

Manual procedure, using audited registered syntax (not claimed as human-executed): `/cosmic combat trace on`, `/cosmic proc trace on`, `/cosmic enchant book give @s cosmicpve:death_coffin 1 100 100` (repeat for II/III and apply to Sword/Axe/Bow/Crossbow), `/cosmic enchant book give @s cosmicpve:pyre 1 100 100` (repeat for II/III and apply to Axe), `/cosmic combat set-health @e[type=minecraft:zombie,sort=nearest,limit=1] 6`, `/cosmic combat trace last`, `/cosmic proc trace last`. Compare a target above 33% with one below; cluster non-allies at 1/2/3 blocks, add an allied party member/owned ally, and fire a Bow/Crossbow then switch weapons before impact. For Pyre, count successful ignitions and confirm ~3-second fire. Use `/cosmic accessory give cinderwolf @s` and `/cosmic accessory give belt_socket @s 100` to socket leggings, attach the Belt, and compare ordinary fire/lava with true/nonfire damage; proc tracing can distinguish the 15% factor from flat points. For the room, `/cosmic trial portal give @s`, place/enter and wait for the Decision Box, then `/cosmic trial debug force-room cinder_wolf`; inspect `/cosmic trial debug status`, all nine hotbar/armor positions, solo 200 HP, first volley ~8 seconds, first wave ~12 seconds, later waves, fire immunity, boss death and Diamond exit. Two participants should yield 350 HP and four Pups/wave. Finish through the gateway (not `debug complete-room`) and inspect the one weighted reward; repetition is needed because weight 5 is not guaranteed. For later-phase testing, progress naturally to Impossible/Demonic and force Cinder Wolf from that phase's Decision Box; the active phase remains authoritative for the normal pool. Record approximate kill time, volley/wave counts, Pup accumulation and damage pressure. Human visual/feel, projectile impact and party/reward acceptance remain pending.

## Trials Creation Kit + Cosmic Swag Bag + Healing + Berserk candidate (2026-09-22)

Status: IMPLEMENTED: YES; AUTOMATED/RUNTIME VERIFIED: YES; MANUALLY VERIFIED/ACCEPTED: YES (user's explicit acceptance in the next milestone brief); COMMITTED: YES in the selective closure commit `Add Trials Creation Kit and Cosmic Swag Bag`. The prior Apprentice PV Unlock row and exact 29-row Flash Sale catalog remain part of the committed baseline. Abandoned Spaceship remains deferred.

The historical manual-check language below records what was still pending before that explicit acceptance; it does not reopen this closed milestone.

Follow-up enchantment copy/fuse patch: Nimble now reads `Proccing a defensive enchantment empowers your next hit!`; Curse, Healing and Self Destruct now use the exact requested shorter descriptions. Self Destruct's four event-owned TNT entities now use a 15-tick (0.75-second) fuse instead of the earlier 20 ticks. The ordinary TNT default remains 80 ticks; owner immunity and terrain-safe explosion policy are unchanged. The focused Step 7D test and all 24 loaded GameTests pass with the new fuse. Earlier milestone notes below that mention 20 ticks describe the historical candidate, not the current behavior.

Trial Trinket application feedback follow-up: the server-side inventory stack-on-Portal bridge sends the existing player-local `PLAYER_LEVELUP` success cue exactly when `TrialTrinketApplicationService` returns `SUCCESS`; rejected/stale/equal-or-weaker applications remain silent. Focused Trial Trinket and shared feedback tests pass. Human playback remains to be checked in-game.

- `cosmicpve:trials_creation_kit` is a nonstackable End Portal Frame-model item with permanent glint, bold `#FA9E05` name, and exact canonical flavor. Its 15 rows are Portal ×1/×2/×3 (weight 10 each); Skip 1/2/3, Time 1/3/5, Fame 33/66/100, and Madness 1/2/3 Trinkets (each variant family weighted 6/4/2). Insurance is absent. Five rolls sample by **row** without replacement, so different Portal quantities may coexist but the same row cannot repeat. Real Trial Portal and Trinket factories construct rewards. The Flash Sale Kit entry is now production-selectable at $2.5m/$2.8m/$3.1m.
- `cosmicpve:cosmic_swag_bag` is a nonstackable Pumpkin-model item with permanent glint, exact bold-italic per-character `#20F5EF/#FFFFFF/#E994F2` cycle, and exact white-italic flavor. Its 29 rows are Simple/Unique/Elite/Ultimate/Legendary Unexamined Books ×1/×2/×3 (weight 5 each); Heroic and Mastery Books ×1 (5 each); a physical Banknote (25); PV Unlock (30); 3k/5k/7.5k/10k XP Bottles (15 each); Transmog (25); White Scroll (50); Repair Scroll ×1/×2 (25 each); 50% Armor/Weapon Orbs (10 each). Three independent weighted draws use replacement. Only after the Money row is selected, its Banknote payload is uniformly sampled in whole dollars from $1 to $1,000,000 inclusive, without rounding or direct balance credit.
- Both items use the existing durable `SingleRewardAnimationService`: all 5/3 final stacks are selected and journaled before source consumption, cosmetic cycling cannot reroll them, all result slots reveal together, the exact 60-tick final hold follows reveal, and manual close/recovery delivers each pending result once with normal overflow handling. The shared menu now centers five results in slots 2–6. Left-click air opens read-only previews titled `Trials Creation Kit Preview` / `Cosmic Swag Bag Preview`; identical payloads with quantity variants collapse to their maximum count. Kit Portal previews at ×3; Bag books at ×3, Repair at ×2 and Banknote at its $1m maximum payload.
- Swag Bag is active via the generic production reward descriptor in exactly four specified sources: Advanced Dense Woodlands weight 10 quantity 1, Conquest Chest weight 4 quantity 1, Impossible Trial weight 4 quantity 1, and Demonic Trial weight 7 quantity 1. Existing other rows and Conquest independent rolls/guarantees are unchanged. Trial-backed downstream systems naturally see the new Trial rows.
- Berserk is ordinary Unique Axe I–V: one percent per level on a committed damaging melee Axe hit, granting vanilla Strength I for level seconds *after* the hit commits. Reprocs refresh Strength I without amplifier stacking. Healing is ordinary Unique Crossbow I–II: fixed 10% on a committed damaging projectile impact whose firing-weapon snapshot is a Crossbow, granting vanilla Absorption I/II (4/8 HP) for 80 ticks. Bow, melee and zero-damage hits fail its central condition. Both use normal Luck-relative `ProcEngine` candidates, effective-enchantment suppression and existing child-hit policy; no second event listener or Table pool entry was added. Both are ordinary random-pool-eligible Unique specs, so existing Unexamined Unique/Enchanter/Tinkerer/Dust/application/Black Scroll/Transmog/capacity paths apply without special cases. Existing armor 10 / weapon 12 absolute capacity ceilings remain unchanged.
- Focused catalog, source, presentation, Banknote, Unique metadata and loaded proc tests pass. `gradlew.bat cleanTest test build --console=plain` passes 741 JUnit tests in 179 suites with zero failures/errors/skips. `runGameTestServer` passes all 24 required GameTests, including real Kit/Bag commands, read-only previews, real item use/journaling, 5/3 menu slots, early-close exactly-once delivery, loaded Berserk/Healing applicability, Luck-adjusted proc dispatch and vanilla Strength/Absorption. Dedicated `runServer` publishes content, loads 1,463 recipes and reaches `Done (1.334s)` without client classloading; its startup-only Gradle session was stopped afterward. Fresh `runClient` reloads `mod/cosmicpve`, starts SoundEngine and builds the item atlas with no missing Kit/Bag model after correcting both vanilla block-model paths. The installed Slightly Improved Font metadata warning is unrelated. Human item appearance, sound and real-world drop cadence are not claimed.
- Loaded GameTests execute `/cosmic containers trials-creation-kit @s` and `/cosmic containers cosmic-swag-bag @s`, plus `/enchanter`; both items also pass a real `/give` and item-use path. The following additional manual test syntax was audited against the actual registered commands, but is not claimed as operator-executed in this run: `/cosmic containers memory-chest @s`; `/cosmic flashsale force trials_creation_kit low`; `/cosmic flashsale force memory_chest low`; `/flashsale preview`; `/flashsale buy`; `/cosmic flashsale status`; `/cosmic money @s add 3000000`; `/cosmic trial portal give @s`; `/cosmic trial trinket give @s skip 2`; `/cosmic conquest spawn-here`; `/cosmic enchant book give @s cosmicpve:berserk 1 100 100` (or level 5); `/cosmic enchant book give @s cosmicpve:healing 1 100 100` (or level 2). Flash Sale purchase and real-world reward selection remain manual acceptance checks.

Check Unexamined Unique Books and `/enchanter` with repeated rolls; neither new enchantment should appear in the Cosmic Enchantment Table. Human visual/behavioral acceptance is still required.

## Cosmic Crates Placeholder + Memory Chests + Higher-Lore Enchantment Orbs candidate (2026-09-20)

- `cosmicpve:memory_chest` is a real nonstackable Ender Chest-based premium item. `Memory` uses the exact bold per-letter colors `BF0F0F/BF0F76/870FBF/0F26BF/0FB0BF/0FBF3E`; ` Chest` is bold white. It is a one-result container, not an ordinary five-result Space Chest.
- Right-click first selects one final reward and journals it through the existing `SingleRewardAnimationService`, then consumes the source and saves the player. Cosmetic frames use the same weighted candidates but cannot change the persisted obligation. The inherited reveal and exact 60-tick final hold, early-close/relogin recovery, exactly-once clearing, inventory-first delivery and overflow drop behavior are unchanged. Left-click air opens the read-only `Memory Chest Preview` with all eleven outcomes and neither consumes nor rolls the source.
- Exact Memory table (unnormalized, total weight 104): Ultimate Space Chest 36×1; Legendary Space Chest 24×1; Mastery Space Chest 12×1; Spring Left/Right, Summer Left/Right, Fall Left/Right and Winter Left/Right Cosmic Crate Halves, each 4×1.
- Every currently materializable canonical Memory source is active through the generic reward descriptor/factory: Advanced Dense Woodlands 6×1; Trial Impossible 5×1; Trial Demonic 4×2. Trial-backed Fame Shop construction therefore works without a special case. Flash Sale has one selectable Memory Chest at low/medium/high prices `$2.5m/$3.5m/$4.5m` (stored as 250m/350m/450m cents). The repository contains no existing Heroic Abandoned Spaceship Lootbag catalog/factory, so its future 6×1 row remains explicitly deferred; no dungeon, portal, lootbag or other Spaceship system was invented.
- Eight nonstackable half IDs are `spring_cosmic_crate_left_half`, `spring_cosmic_crate_right_half`, `summer_cosmic_crate_left_half`, `summer_cosmic_crate_right_half`, `fall_cosmic_crate_left_half`, `fall_cosmic_crate_right_half`, `winter_cosmic_crate_left_half`, and `winter_cosmic_crate_right_half`. Four inert full IDs are `spring_cosmic_crate`, `summer_cosmic_crate`, `fall_cosmic_crate`, and `winter_cosmic_crate`.
- Full crates reference the vanilla Note Block block model. All seasons share two code-native half-block models: Left occupies model X `0..8`, Right occupies X `8..16`, with the other horizontal half absent/transparent. Each half remains a distinct season/side item. The client resource reload reports no missing CosmicPVE model/texture/particle reference.
- Primary-click inventory stacking is server-authoritative. Exactly one matching opposite half of the same season on cursor/slot is consumed and replaced in the interacted slot by exactly one full crate; direction is symmetric. Same-side, cross-season and unrelated items are not intercepted. A successful transaction emits the normal level-up cue once. Stale/repeated packets cannot consume the completed crate because it is no longer a half. No crafting recipe exists.
- Complete crates are intentionally inert placeholders: right-click returns PASS, they are not `LootPreviewProvider`s and no seasonal reward factory exists. Their name is `*** COSMIC CRATE: <Season> ***`; opening/label are white with bold `COSMIC CRATE:`, while season and closing stars use Spring `#72B545`, Summer `#F0CA60`, Fall `#BA8807`, Winter `#5ED1D6`. Lore is exactly blank, gold `TREASURE ITEMS` + nine gold `* ` rows, blank, blue `BONUS ITEMS (1)` + four blue `* ` rows, blank, and the single red `Ensure you have a lot of free space!` warning. There is no owner/shop/Admin/Cosmetics/placement/open-area text.

Their displayed names are `Armor Enchantment Orb [9]`, `Armor Enchantment Orb [10]`, `Weapon Enchantment Orb [11]`, and `Weapon Enchantment Orb [12]`. ## Dense Woodlands 2.0+ candidate (2026-09-19)

- Accepted closure: Deathbringer + Planetary Deathbringer + General Cleanup was selectively committed as `9d372ec37f16cb8ff4d627faf8c10e8bf6995b97` (`Implement Deathbringer and general cleanup`). `docs/Cosmic_Design.md` and root `assets`, `blockbench`, and `woodlands` remained outside commit ownership.
- Pinpoint is a real Elite Bow enchantment, max VI, with a normal Luck-relative 3% per-level committed-hit proc that applies Weakness I for 80 ticks. `randomPoolEligible=false` excludes its initial generation systemically from generic books, Enchanter/Table previews, V-Kits and generated equipment while preserving normal explicit books, application, Dust, Tinkerer, Transmog, capacity, extraction, suppression and combat behavior.
- Supplied source assets were copied byte-for-byte to `cosmicpve:woodlands/woodlands_small_tree4`, `cosmicpve:woodlands/woodlands_tall_tree3`, and `cosmicpve:woodlands/woodlands_arena`. Short/tall pools now expose four/three equally selectable variants without changing their placement frequencies. Campsite rarity changed from 48 to 69, the equivalent area adjustment for approximately 20% greater linear spacing in the existing rarity-filter architecture.
- `cosmicpve:woodlands_arena` is a real jigsaw structure limited to Dense Woodlands, backed by its own template pool and random-spread structure set (`spacing=64`, `separation=16`, `salt=1847365297`). The arena asset contains its central Lectern and exactly four Light markers. Its projected jigsaw `start_height` remains `absolute=-1`, so every newly generated arena (including Lectern, markers, decorations, rotation and realized bounds) is exactly one block below the former terrain-anchor result; existing starts are not migrated. The severe rectangular dirt/stone cuts came from projecting one large rigid pool element onto `WORLD_SURFACE_WG` while the structure itself declared no terrain adaptation. The structure now uses native jigsaw `terrain_adaptation: beard_thin`, matching vanilla surface structures such as villages/outposts: this adjusts surrounding worldgen density to blend the exterior while the pool element deliberately remains `projection: rigid`, so the authored floor, Lectern, Light markers and interior are not vertically warped. No second Y offset or pool-element `terrain_matching` processor was added.
- The follow-up circular-footprint hotfix fixes the remaining square cutout at its actual source. The 37x16x37 template's authored geometry reaches a maximum horizontal radius squared of 325 from `(18,18)`; 5,376 explicit Air cells beyond that circle are now Structure Voids, so they preserve the generated terrain instead of excavating it. The 15,309 authored Air cells inside the circular envelope remain Air and still clear the arena interior. Filling the exterior with Grass was deliberately rejected because it would create another artificial level pad rather than preserve local terrain. This affects newly generated starts only.
- Every custom tree family—small, tall and fallen—now computes its complete rotated template bounding box before placement and rejects placement when that box horizontally intersects any realized Woodlands Arena `StructureStart` bounds. The comparison deliberately ignores Y, preventing trunks, crowns and fallen logs from entering the arena footprint at any height. Non-tree template families remain unchanged.
- Nine canonical Dense Woodlands Scraps in a full 3x3 recipe produce `cosmicpve:desecrated_tomb_ranger`. The custom item carries actual Written Book content, author/title/page metadata, permanent glint, bold `#43B03C` name and concise instructions, and is accepted by vanilla Lecterns through the item tag.
- Arena summoning validates the actual generated structure containing the Lectern, scopes marker lookup to that structure bounding box, requires exactly four markers, rejects a second living Ranger in the same arena without consuming the Tomb, and consumes/clears one Tomb only after a successful spawn. Arenas are reusable after the boss dies.
- `cosmicpve:cosmic_ranger` uses Bogged behavior/rendering at 1.25 scale, is persistent and ranged-only, and has 350 health, 3 armor, 1 toughness and 0.28 movement speed. Its undroppable full Heroic Iron Ranger set, Protection IV, exact Cosmic armor enchantments (including Leadership X), and undroppable Power V/Flame I plus exact Cosmic Bow enchantments are built through the real item/enchantment systems.
- The Ranger persists explicit arena/marker/wave state. It now also owns and persists the exact realized `StructureStart.getBoundingBox()`, its most recent valid position, bound participant UUID/last-valid positions, and phase-add last-valid positions. Outward Ranger motion is suppressed against those actual six faces before movement where possible; a server-authoritative post-tick guard restores the Ranger to its last legal position, zeros motion/fall distance and stops navigation without changing HP, phase, effects, equipment, target or encounter identity. There is no radius approximation, asset-size approximation, block barrier or global lock.
- The legitimate summoner is bound immediately after a successful spawn. Any other player becomes a participant only while their saved Adventure is ACTIVE and they are legitimately inside that same placed arena; an outside player is never pulled in. Bound participants may move normally inside, but any horizontal/vertical escape or teleport is restored to that participant's recent in-bounds position with motion/fall distance cleared. A dead player or player whose Adventure is no longer ACTIVE is removed before correction, so death, timeout, extraction, return and recovery take precedence. Removing/killing the Ranger removes the only enforcement owner; separate Rangers retain independent bounds/maps. State survives entity save/reload.
- Overkill is clamped after the full damage pipeline so 66% and 33% waves cannot be skipped. Each threshold spawns four canonical Forest Fanatic/Dreadmane jockey pairs at the four markers, tracks both halves until dead, and records both as Ranger-owned allies. Ranger-owned root adds are guarded by the same actual bounds and restored without changing HP/ownership or counting as dead; mounted riders follow their corrected Dreadmane. While a wave lives, all ordinary damage paths are rejected and the exact player warning is throttled to 60 ticks per player; invulnerability-bypass administration remains available.
- Forest Fanatic and Dreadmane now participate in the persisted ownership system and refuse hostile targeting of their owner/owned allies. Dense Woodlands Fanatics clear inherited daylight fire only in that dimension, fixing valid daytime jockeys without changing outside-dimension skeleton behavior.
- Ordinary Forest Fanatics now override only vanilla's base XP amount with one uniform inclusive 15-20 roll; ordinary Dreadmanes do the same with one uniform inclusive 25-35 roll. Vanilla eligibility, orb spawning, death attribution, independent two-entity Jockey rewards, the 25%/40% item-table rolls and Ranger-add suppression remain separate and unchanged. Dreadmane Buck now directly uses one 11-damage packet; normal melee remains 7.5 and its collision-box range, rear animation, 1.5 knockback, Slowness II/35 ticks and success-only 200-tick cooldown are unchanged.
- Self Destruct now sets `PrimedTnt#setFuse(20)` only on its four tagged event-owned TNT entities. The strict below-15% threshold, four cardinal offsets, owner immunity, cleared affected-block list and 1,200-tick cooldown are unchanged; unrelated TNT retains vanilla's 80-tick default.
- Ranger death suppresses Bogged/equipment loot and still performs exactly four independent rolls from `cosmicpve:adventure/advanced_dense_woodlands`, spawning preserved stacks five blocks north/east/south/west with duplicates allowed. The current canonical 22 rows, in order as weight × quantity, are: Simple Unexamined Book 6×3; Unique 6×3; Elite 6×3; Random V-Kit Crystal 5×1; Ultimate/Legendary/Mastery Space Chests 8/6/4×1; 100% Ranger Crystal 5×1; 20k and 30k XP Bottles 5×1 each; 100% Armor and Weapon Orbs 5×1 each; Secret Weapon Cache 4×1; Skip 2 Portal 4×1; +3 Madness Ballot Portal 4×1; Mystery Mastery Spawner 5×1; Heroic Crystal 5×1; Random Ranger Armor 15×1; 75% Ranger Crystal 8×1; Space Dust Bundle 5×2; $1m-$4m Banknote 7×1; Pinpoint VI random Success/Destroy 7×1. Both Ranger Crystal rows use the real `armor_set_crystal` factory and store exact success 100/75. Banknotes remain physical items and uniformly select an inclusive $1,000,000-$4,000,000 value in $10,000 increments. Removed stale rows are 10k XP and Cosmic Enchantment Table; old book weights 8, armor weight 10, Dust weight 7, banknote weight 10/range $1m-$2m are superseded.
- Circuit Circus now supplies every participant 16 temporary Baked Potatoes in hotbar slot 9 (inventory index 8), alongside the unchanged Infinity Bow and Arrow. It deliberately does not use Bread; the normal room loadout clear/transition path removes leftovers.
- `/adventures`, `/adventure`, and `/adv` open one read-only 27-slot Adventures menu with the three canonical entries at slots 11/13/15. The nested operator `/cosmic adventure ...` diagnostic surface remains separate.
- Automated evidence: final `gradlew.bat cleanTest test build` passes 727 tests / 176 suites with zero failures, errors or skips, and the normal loaded suite passes all 24 GameTests. Focused coverage asserts `beard_thin`, the unchanged single `absolute=-1` anchor and rigid pool projection; exact exterior Structure Void/interior Air counts and the circular radius boundary; all three tree-family classifications and Y-independent horizontal-bound intersection; the exact 22-row Advanced catalog/weights/quantities and stale-row absence; exact inclusive XP mappings; Buck's unchanged 7.5 melee and one direct 11-damage packet for both an ordinary Dreadmane and a real two-entity Jockey, plus range replacement/cooldown/knockback/Slowness/animation; and actual Self Destruct entities at 20 fuse ticks, no early detonation, four completed detonations, caster immunity, retained dirt and unrelated TNT at 80 ticks. Existing Pinpoint, tree, lookup, summon, arena-bound persistence/containment, Ranger phase/drop-count, Circuit, Trial and cleanup coverage remains green. The six opt-in Adventure GameTests still cannot instantiate custom dimensions under `GameTestServer`; five existing dimension-dependent tests fail for that environment limitation and are not counted as regressions.
- Runtime evidence: a fresh fixed-seed dedicated world with structures enabled loaded 1,463 recipes, published the exact Advanced table and reached `Done`. Four real Dense Woodlands jigsaw starts were then generated through the bounded production locator from origins around `(0,0)`, `(10000,0)`, `(0,10000)` and `(-10000,-10000)`. Verified Lecterns were `(350,76,158)`, `(10606,75,-18)`, `(162,81,9634)` and `(-10162,72,-9774)` with realized 61×40×61 bounds beginning at Y 62, 61, 67 and 58 respectively. A first full-height scan correctly exposed log-only fallen-tree placements, leading to the all-family correction. After regenerating the world from the same seed, all four complete 61×61 effective footprints were force-loaded and scanned from Y -64 through 319: zero blocks in either `#minecraft:logs` or `#minecraft:leaves` remained. The server saved all dimensions and stopped normally. This proves decoding, placement, bounded generation, preserved authored landmarks and tree exclusion across varied elevations, but does not claim human visual judgment of the exterior transitions. A fresh isolated client reloaded `mod/cosmicpve`, initialized OpenAL/SoundEngine and built block/item/GUI/decorated-pot atlases without a new CosmicPVE resource, model, texture, codec or sided-loading failure; the owned smoke process stopped cleanly with a successful Gradle task.
- Locate hotfix: manual testing found vanilla `/locate structure cosmicpve:woodlands_arena` could monopolize the integrated server for minutes. The first replacement incorrectly reported the random-spread start-chunk corner without validating or resolving the rotated jigsaw bounds; a follow-up lookup also failed on untouched dimensions because it queried the structure manager before the candidate start existed. `/cosmic adventure locate-arena` now reads the registered seeded 64/16 grid directly, always against Dense Woodlands, checks a bounded 5x5 neighboring-region set at `STRUCTURE_STARTS`, then probes four bounded recovery rings for legacy/finalized chunks that predate the arena. It skips invalid starts, fully generates only the first real arena's bounded footprint, verifies the authored Lectern block, and reports that exact Lectern coordinate plus the realized bounds and candidate count. A clean dedicated-world proof using the user's exact world seed and last observed coordinates completed in about 1.5 seconds, found the arena 331 blocks away, and independently confirmed `minecraft:lectern` at the returned position. Vanilla `/locate` is no longer the recommended arena test route.
- Adventure-entry hotfix: switching integrated-server worlds in one client process could retain the prior world's process-global `ActivityType.ADVENTURE` for the same offline player UUID. A genuinely empty new world's Call was consequently rejected by the old generic “already active or unavailable” message. Login and Call activation now clear only an orphaned Adventure activity when the current server has no matching saved Adventure session. Real blockers remain enforced, but the Call now reports the precise reason (existing Adventure, dead/spectator, already in Woodlands, missing dimension, active Trial, another activity, or invalid held Call). Runtime GameTest coverage proves orphaned activity self-healing and non-consuming precise rejection for real activity.
- Structure-generation diagnosis: the user's replacement world was created with `generate_features=0` (`WorldOptions.generateStructures()==false`). This globally prevents both vanilla and CosmicPVE structure starts, so neither locator can find an arena regardless of dimension regeneration. `/cosmic adventure locate-arena` now reports this exact world-setting error instead of the misleading no-start message. Arena generation requires a world created with **Generate Structures: ON**.
- Direct Ranger testing: outside `cosmicpve:dense_woodlands`, a summoned Cosmic Ranger no longer needs arena Light markers for its threshold waves. Each wave spawns exactly four complete Dreadmane/Forest Fanatic jockey pairs at the Ranger's current Y, four blocks north/east/south/west. Dense Woodlands encounters continue to use the four authored arena markers. The loaded entity GameTest asserts all four exact cardinal mount positions plus eight tracked/owned wave members.
- Manual acceptance and closure: the user completed the Dense Woodlands 2.0 testing cycle and explicitly accepted the candidate, including its final arena terrain/footprint and tree-exclusion hotfixes, on 2026-09-20. The complete candidate is closed in the dedicated `Implement Dense Woodlands 2.0` commit containing this status update. Existing generated arena starts remain non-migrating; future visual comparisons must still use regenerated or unexplored starts.

Teleport to the reported Lectern coordinate, not the old start-chunk corner.

Repeat against the mount of a natural Woodlands Jockey.

## Deathbringer / General Cleanup candidate (2026-09-17)

### Follow-up fixes: Amulet presentation / Woodlands diagnosis / compact previews

- All three Amulet wrappers now use `flip_v=false`: the exporter writes top-down Blockbench UVs, and the previous flip mirrored sampling into unrelated palette regions (same cause as Shock Therapy). Generated OBJ geometry is centered on all three axes for inventory/chest/hand contexts. Each FIXED transform holds its separate existing body-local worn offset; inventory definitions no longer request oversized GUI rendering. Authored Blockbench models/textures are unchanged. Regression coverage checks every authored cube/mesh UV and centered bounds.
- Actual Dense Woodlands dedicated-server proof: native surface-position calls produced mobs with the loaded 70/30 biome pool; after cleanup, 200 ordinary server ticks automatically produced 70 hostiles across 289 spawnable chunks. No new scheduler, forced mob cap, or bypass spawn policy was introduced. Opt-in `-Dcosmicpve.woodlandsSpawnProbe=true` runs this development probe, cleans up its entities/player and stops that dedicated server.
- Read-only inspection of local `run/saves/New World (5)/level.dat` found Normal difficulty, `minecraft:spawn_monsters=true`, but `minecraft:spawn_mobs=false`. This disables natural spawning, including Dense Woodlands. The user's saved world was NOT modified. Runtime-verified operator fix: `/gamerule minecraft:spawn_mobs true`. This is a world-wide gamerule, so apply deliberately; the mod does not silently override it. `/cosmic adventure inspect` now also reports both spawn gamerules, difficulty, spawnable chunks, category counts and player dimension.
- Preview identity rule: enchantment + level + Success option. Random Destroy variations do not create additional entries. Cosmic Table now has 53 entries / one page; Heroic Table has 36 / one page. Preview-only tooltip shows `Destroy Rate: 1–100% (random)` or `51–100% (random)` for Mastery rather than suggesting a fixed reward rate. Actual reward selection/rates and read-only pagination for genuinely large pools are unchanged.
- Follow-up `cleanTest test build` passes 713 tests / 174 suites, zero failures/errors. All 23 required GameTests pass with current compact preview assertions. Repeated real-dimension probes produced 70 and 72 automatic hostiles at 200 ticks (289 spawnable chunks), and the exact re-enable command was runtime verified. Client reload after the Amulet changes initialized item/GUI atlases and sound without new model-load errors; only the owned startup client was stopped afterward. Visual worn/inventory acceptance remains pending; native automatic spawning is now demonstrated in the real generated dimension, rather than inferred from entity fixtures.

Scope and implementation:

### Final hotfix: current Trial catalogs / Secret Weapon Cache / Belt GUI / emergency Pots

- Four Trial tables and their declarations now exactly match the current Design Doc's `Canonical Reward Catalogs` section (a source-parity regression compares every row/name/weight/quantity). Apprentice: 14 active rows, weight 101; Hardcore: 13 active of 14 declared, active weight 82 / declared 87; Impossible: 20 active of 21 declared, 175 / 183; Demonic: 18 active of 19 declared, 133 / 137. Only the unavailable Abandoned Spaceship/Heroic Spaceship portals remain inactive; no future content registration was invented and the live Design Doc was not edited.
- New validated `random_trial_trinket` descriptor (`trinket_tier` 1–3) selects equally among the five real types, including Madness, and generates correct tier-specific Time 1/3/5, Fame 33/66/100 and Skip/Insurance/Madness 1/2/3. `accessory_socket` descriptor validates the three real socket items and rate 1–100, constructing exact component-bearing 25/65% Amulet/Belt and 50% Omni rewards instead of silently using their 100% item defaults. Production row construction/all fifteen trinket outcomes/exact socket components are verified in the loaded GameTest.
- Secret Weapon Cache root cause: its six signature candidates were passed to the Weapon Skin Generator's five-outcome-only selector. A separate uniform six-weapon selector fixes opening without weakening the skin pool's contract. Loaded test exercises actual item right-click, source consumption, one persisted signature reward, early-close delivery and duplicate-close safety. Unit test reaches all six index outcomes and verifies returned stacks are copies.
- Bandolier and Shock Therapy GUI Z rotation is now 180 degrees. Only GUI transforms change; accepted FIXED/worn and held transforms, texture UVs and geometry remain untouched by this orientation hotfix. Manual inventory/chest appearance still needs user acceptance.
- EMERGENCY Woodlands decorated pots: common-side HIGHEST right-click handler blocks both main/off-hand deposits for noncreative/nonspectator players in Dense Woodlands. Empty main-hand right-click instead returns stored contents, clearing the pot before delivery; item identity/components/count survive, repeated clicks cannot duplicate, inventory overflow drops safely, and the pot is never broken. Creative debug use, ordinary dimensions and Trial Cave Diving puzzle pots are unchanged. This also rescues preexisting stored items after restart; no saved-world edits or bulk pot clearing occur.
- Real generated-dimension Adventure-mode runtime proof verifies both deposit paths, named/enchanted/damaged sword recovery, exactly-once delivery, intact pot and safe full-inventory overflow. Native automatic spawning remains functional (73 native hostiles at 200 ticks in this fresh verification world).
