### Amulet Socket + Blood Diamond/Icicle/Black Heart — IMPLEMENTED: YES; AUTOMATED/RUNTIME VERIFIED: YES; MANUALLY VERIFIED/ACCEPTED: YES; COMMITTED: YES

The accepted Overload/Silence/Longbow and content-hotpatch candidate was selectively closed as `30771fc` (`Add Overload Silence Longbow and content hotfixes`). Its owned tracked source, resource, test, and handoff files were included; the user-maintained `docs/Cosmic_Design.md` and root `assets/`, `blockbench/`, and `woodlands/` development sources were excluded and preserved.

The accessory foundation uses three synchronized, persistent item components: versioned `AccessoryLoadout` on host equipment, versioned `AccessorySocketData` on socket items, and versioned `AccessoryItemData` on loose accessories. At this milestone's closure, `AccessorySlot` exposed active `AMULET` while reserving `BELT` for the next bounded candidate; the Belt/Omni section below records that subsequent implementation. A loadout stores concrete unlocked slots separately from a slot-to-accessory identity map, validates that every attachment has its socket, and permits at most one identity per slot. It remains a component of the original chestplate, so ordinary component-preserving enchantment, Heroic, Armor Set, Omni, Transmog, White/Holy Scroll, Orb, durability, repair, rename, copy/save/drop/dimension operations do not replace or discard it.

`cosmicpve:amulet_socket` carries an exact integer 1–100 success rate and no destroy field. It uses the vanilla Tripwire Hook item model and the canonical Socket color `#055251` for its bold item name, success text, and flavor text. Only real chest armor is accepted: the chest armor tag or chest armor-attribute fallback is required in addition to chest-slot equipability, which rejects Elytra and arbitrary chest-slot items. A valid attempt consumes one Socket exactly once. A 100% item succeeds without querying RNG; a failed roll leaves the chestplate bit-for-bit unchanged and uses survived-failure feedback, never anvil-break/destruction feedback. Reapplication to an already socketed chestplate rejects before RNG and consumption. The exact flavor text is `Apply to any CHESTPLATE to gain the ability to wear amulets! Drag n'drop to apply!`; its tooltip also renders `Success Rate: N%` and no Destroy Rate.

Loose Blood Diamond, Icicle, and Black Heart items attach through the normal primary inventory stack-on-item interaction only after the Amulet socket exists. Attachment has no roll, consumes one loose item, and rejects a second without overwriting or consuming it. Empty-cursor secondary interaction returns exactly one canonical loose Amulet while retaining the permanent socket. Stale cursor/slot object identities reject transaction races. The host tooltip now uses canonical Socket presentation: `Socketed: EMPTY` in bold `#055251`, or `Socketed: Amulet: …` with the bold `#055251` prefix and the attached identity in its canonical Amulet color, plus the removal instruction. Loose names are entirely bold and use Blood Diamond `#5C0404`, Icicle `#BDF0FF`, and Black Heart `#310082`, followed by the canonical concise effect and attach instruction.

Canonical source-to-gameplay mappings are:

- `blockbench/amulets/blood_diamond/blood_diamond.bbmodel` (`cosmic_ruby_amulet`) → Blood Diamond.
- `blockbench/amulets/icicle/icicle.bbmodel` (`cosmic_frost_amulet`) → Icicle.
- `blockbench/amulets/blackened_heart/blackened_heart.bbmodel` (`cosmic_void_amulet`) → Black Heart.

The source files remain untouched/untracked user assets. Reproducible `tools/export_amulet_models.ps1` reads their embedded 32×32 textures and authored cubes/mesh, rotations, pivots, and UVs, producing namespaced OBJ/MTL/PNG runtime assets. After the first manual visual pass exposed a shared offset, the exporter was corrected to retain torso-local geometry while adding Minecraft's conventional `(0.5, 0.5, 0.5)` model-center offset. This prevents the item renderer's own center translation from shifting all models left/up. Model wrappers include explicit texture/particle aliases, a centered GUI view, and explicit first-/third-person transforms that anchor held Amulets to the hand. Client-only `AmuletClientPresentation` captures the equipped chestplate identity into `AvatarRenderState` and adds a dedicated layer for every player skin. The layer follows `PlayerModel.body`, submits the fixed model over existing chest armor, skips invisible avatars, and never replaces armor/model/stats. Common registration never references the client class; dedicated-server startup verifies sided safety. A fresh client reload after the correction reports no Amulet or Socket missing model/texture warning. Exact final physical placement, clipping, animation, and compatibility with each appearance remain manual acceptance items.

`AccessoryResolver` is the one equipped-accessory query boundary and reads only the actual chest equipment slot for any `LivingEntity`; inventory, hotbar, containers, and dropped hosts are inactive. Accessories are not Cosmic enchantments and do not participate in capacity, Heroic conversion, Silence, Neutralize, Grim Axe, Black Scroll, or Enchanted Black Scroll suppression.

`AmuletCombatService` is the one combat integration point. Blood Diamond reads canonical active `cosmicpve:bleed` stack counts on attacker and target during ordinary root outgoing resolution and contributes +1% per stack to the shared additive bucket; true, status, proc, retaliation, and child damage are excluded. Icicle produces one root-only, once-per-event `ON_VALID_HIT` candidate only for a committed ordinary parent hit against a currently Slowed living target. Its exact 10% classification ignores Luck and all other chance multipliers; success uses the canonical Bleed stack service, cap, duration, attribution, and movement reconciliation to add one standard stack.

Black Heart is implemented as generic successful-proc observation rather than an enchantment allowlist. `ProcActivation` now collects the living entities an action actually affected, and current Mastery/Heroic damage/debuff actions mark accepted results. The listener resolves rarity from `CosmicEnchantmentSpecs`, requires Mastery or Heroic, another owner, the wearer among actual affected entities, and an equipped Black Heart. Failed, suppressed, passive, self-only, owner-self, and irrelevant lower-rarity cases never qualify. Runtime weak state stores one expiry per entity: a qualification sets current tick + 80, a retrigger replaces that expiry, magnitude remains +5%, and the shared ordinary root bucket excludes true/status/proc/child damage.

Tinkerer salvage rejects valid loadouts with any attached accessory, preventing Amulet destruction. A socket-only enchanted chestplate remains eligible under normal salvage rules. Deterministic coverage explicitly guards both cases.

Final automated/runtime verification: focused Amulet and salvage suites pass; `gradlew.bat cleanTest test build --console=plain` passes 670 JUnit tests across 166 suites with zero failures, errors, or skips. `gradlew.bat runGameTestServer --console=plain` passes all 18 required loaded-registry GameTests, including the new Amulet environment's live socket/attachment/equipped-only resolution, Blood Diamond 3+5 calculation, fixed Icicle chance and canonical Bleed, child exclusion, and Black Heart Mastery activation/80-tick refresh/+5% ordinary-only behavior. Dedicated `runServer` reaches `Done (0.558s)` without client classloading; the verification harness required Ctrl-C termination after startup because Gradle's PTY did not accept the normal stop command. Fresh `runClient` reloads `mod/cosmicpve`, starts the sound engine, and builds item and all other texture atlases without a CosmicPVE missing model/texture, OBJ, registry, codec, localization, or sided-loading error. The installed Slightly Improved Font pack retains its unrelated metadata warning. Candidate-owned paths pass whitespace checks; `docs/Cosmic_Design.md` retains its pre-existing user diff and is excluded. The Amulet candidate was subsequently manually accepted and selectively committed as `b55f329` (`Add amulet sockets and amulets`).

Permissioned manual acquisition commands:

- `/cosmic accessory give socket @s 100` and `/cosmic accessory give socket @s 1` for convenient success/failure sampling.
- `/cosmic accessory give blood_diamond @s`
- `/cosmic accessory give icicle @s`
- `/cosmic accessory give black_heart @s`
- `/give @s minecraft:diamond_chestplate`, plus representative normal, Armor Set/Omni, and Heroic-appearance chestplates from their existing test flows.

Manual acceptance must inspect exact Socket flavor/success display, successful and failed drag application, already-socketed rejection, each attach/remove/re-attach cycle and permanent socket, concise host identities, front/side/back placement while standing/walking/crouching, and overlay compatibility with no armor/ordinary/Armor Set/Omni/Heroic appearances. Combat checks should use `/cosmic combat trace on`, `/cosmic proc trace on`, `/cosmic combat trace last`, `/cosmic proc trace last`, and existing stack/effect commands to validate known Blood Diamond Bleed totals, fixed-rate Icicle hits against Slowness, and Black Heart against representative hostile Permafrost/Mighty Cactus-style Mastery/Heroic actions. No force-proc command was invented; sample real rolls where existing commands cannot deterministically force the production action.

### Belt Socket + Bandolier/Shock Therapy/Jelly Roll + Omni Socket — IMPLEMENTED: YES; AUTOMATED/RUNTIME VERIFIED: YES; MANUALLY VERIFIED/ACCEPTED: YES; COMMITTED: YES (`c67d13c`)

Manual acceptance: the latest milestone brief explicitly records the user's confirmation, “Belts finally work.” This supersedes the prior pending visual-acceptance statements below; Jelly Roll's missing canonical source art remains the documented placeholder limitation.

The shared accessory foundation now treats `AMULET` and `BELT` as active concrete slots. `AccessoryApplicationService` performs one atomic socket/attach/remove transaction for both families, and `AccessoryResolver` reads Belts only from the actually equipped leggings slot. `cosmicpve:belt_socket` stores a fixed 1–100 success rate in the existing versioned concrete-socket component and accepts only real leggings; failure consumes one Socket but never changes or destroys the host, while an existing Belt socket rejects before RNG or consumption. `cosmicpve:omni_socket` stores only its pre-application success rate in `omni_socket_success`; chestplate success installs `AMULET`, leggings success installs `BELT`, and no `OMNI` enum/loadout/armor origin state exists. Both items use the Tripwire Hook model, bold `#055251` names, and exact teal success/flavor presentation.

Shock Therapy, Bandolier, and Jelly Roll are registered as bold, color-canonical loose items and use the accepted primary attach/empty-cursor secondary remove interaction. Host data retains the permanent Belt socket independently from at most one attached Belt identity. Tooltips use bold teal `Socketed: EMPTY`, or a bold teal `Socketed: ` prefix followed by the Belt's own colored `Belt: …` identity. Because this data remains on the original host component, ordinary component-preserving enchantment, Heroic, Armor Set, Omni Armor, Transmog, White/Holy Scroll, Orb/capacity, durability/repair, rename, copy/save/drop, relog, death, and dimension paths preserve it without a parallel transform hook. Tinkerer continues to reject any valid nonempty accessory attachment map, so attached Belts are protected while an empty Belt socket alone remains salvageable.

`BeltCombatService` owns the ordinary damage integrations. Bandolier contributes exactly `+0.12` to the standard additive ordinary outgoing bucket only when transient state is charged and the prospective packet is an ordinary root melee hit whose snapshotted source is in the Sword tag. Only a positive committed matching packet advances state; hits one through three charge it, the committed boosted fourth resets it, and rejected/zero/child/true/projectile/non-Sword packets do not consume it. A player tick reconciles unequip/Belt-change state and death clears it; the counter is intentionally not serialized. Jelly Roll contributes exactly `0.98` through `IncomingDamageContributor` for all ordinary defender-owned sources, including unattributed damage, and contributes nothing to true/execution delivery. After a successfully committed Devour activation and normal `DevourBehavior.commit`, it multiplies the resulting current saturation by `1.20`, clamps it to current hunger, and leaves zero at zero without changing Devour's hunger, damage, heal, sound, chance, or prerequisites.

`CosmicLightningService` is the semantic delivery boundary for every currently implemented owned Cosmic lightning effect discovered in source: Lightning, Stormcaller, and Stormbringer. It extends the original effect's one `NO_PROCS` true packet by exactly `+1.0` when the owner currently wears Shock Therapy, preserving original source identity and attribution rather than creating a second root attack. An accepted resolution heals that owner `0.25 HP` at most once per `ProcActivation`. The service centrally rejects Zeus-equipped targets before base or extension delivery, which also prevents the heal. Vanilla weather/command/environmental bolts never enter this service.

The reproducible `tools/export_belt_models.ps1` maps `blockbench/belts/bandolier/bandolier_belt.bbmodel` to Bandolier and `blockbench/belts/shock_therapy/Shock_Therapy.bbmodel` to Shock Therapy. It preserves authored cube pivots, rotations, face UVs, native texture resolution, and front `-Z`, and emits namespaced OBJ/MTL/PNG resources with explicit item-context transforms. Exported geometry stays centered around Minecraft's `(0.5, 0.5, 0.5)` item-model center (`Shock Therapy Y 0.36..0.64`; `Bandolier Y 0.38..0.60`). Only the worn layer applies the `+12/16 Y` waist offset after following `PlayerModel.body`, leaving the working inventory centering intact. Inspection of pinned `LivingEntityRenderer`, `HumanoidModel`, and `ItemTransform` confirms that player layers already use positive down-body Y and item transforms remove the half-unit model center. The previous negative-offset/armor-occlusion explanation was incorrect and is superseded: it moved the Belt near the head. The red/white-only appearance was a separate UV error: the exporter emits top-down Blockbench UVs, so both Belt wrappers now use `flip_v:false`; the prior true setting mirrored brown/gray bottom palette strips into the top red/white sigil. A regression test samples every exported face's texture region against its authored UV region for both Belts. Physical placement and colors still require a fresh manual check; source/automated checks do not constitute visual acceptance. The layer overlays, rather than replaces, leggings. Candy Buckle sources remain untouched. No canonical Jelly Roll model/texture was supplied, so its loose item remains a visible Leather placeholder and its worn layer is omitted until source art is supplied.

- `gradlew.bat build --console=plain` succeeds, including packaging and Trial structure verification.
- `gradlew.bat runGameTestServer --console=plain` passes all 19 required loaded-registry GameTests. The new Belt test proves equipped-only lookup, loaded Sword classification, hits 1–3/fourth/fifth Bandolier transitions, non-Sword charge preservation, unequip reset, Jelly's ordinary-only `0.98`, exact 5→6 saturation multiplier and zero preservation, and Shock Therapy's exact extension/heal semantics. It also invokes the real Lightning, Stormcaller, and Stormbringer actions and proves each routes its canonical base damage plus exactly one Shock damage through the shared service; the shared multi-target check proves 10→10.25 healing is limited to once across two accepted targets in one activation. The first run exposed and led to correction of the initial Bandolier no-entry transition; every full rerun passed.

### Ten Madness modifiers + Trial progression/data/preset candidate

IMPLEMENTED: accepted milestone plus both bounded hotfixes. AUTOMATED VERIFIED: 706 JUnit tests / 173 suites, zero failures/errors/skips; 22 required GameTests pass. RUNTIME VERIFIED: registered commands, real server menu/packet paths with connected mock players, contributor/engine damage composition, typed reward/Fame Shop materialization, and exact sound dispatch; dedicated startup and client resource/audio initialization smoke checks. ACCEPTED: YES, explicitly by the subsequent Deathbringer milestone brief. COMMITTED: YES, selective closure commit `Refactor trials and add Madness modifiers`. This acceptance does not expand automated evidence: physical room 11 placement and human visual/audio checks were not performed by the headless fixture.

Prior Belt closure: `c67d13c07f680969a9b54b37f77b63a764c5d6c8`, with the focused Belt test passing before selective commit. The design document and root source-art folders were excluded.

`TrialProgression` is the shared ordinal/phase/boundary authority. Next ordinal 1–4 is Apprentice, 5–8 Hardcore, 9–12 Impossible, 13+ Demonic. `TrialProgress` normalizes its legacy serialized phase from completed count. Debug force-phase now changes ordinal progression to the target phase rather than creating contradictory phase/count state. Physical clears award 600/300/0/0 ticks plus 2,400 ticks per newly crossed phase boundary. Skips append phase-tagged acquired reward entries one ordinal at a time, with zero Fame, no ordinary clear time, no room selection, no appearances, and no last-room mutation. Publication and `initialSkipProcessed` remain atomic/idempotent; failed preparation leaves the original session unchanged.

Skip 10: four Apprentice, four Hardcore, two Impossible reward positions; completed 10; next 11; Impossible; base timer 12,000 + 4,800 = 16,800 ticks (840 seconds); two pending Madness selections; no actual-room history changes. Skip 13 naturally adds four rewards from each first-three phase pools and one Demonic reward, crosses three entry bonuses, and starts at ordinal 14 with two pending selections. Debug status reports skipped reward provenance explicitly.

Portal component version 3 stores `time_bonus_seconds`, `skip_rooms`, `insurance_items`, `fame_percent`, and `madness_option_bonus`. Legacy minute/insurance fields and existing Java constructors migrate without losing Fame. Limits are 86,400 time seconds, 1,000 Skip, 1,000 Insurance, 100,000 Fame percent, and 1,000 ballot bonus. Physical trinkets remain curated and stronger-replaces-weaker, with three new Bonus Madness Ballot items in `#8C1708`. Effective choices are `min(remaining, 5, 2+bonus)`. Fame applies once to the final accumulated base pot with integer floor; overflow rejects cashout without mutation. Insurance still selects acquired pot entries without replacement, now up to the numeric safe bound.

Reloadable `data/<namespace>/cosmicpve/madness/*.json` owns stable ID, name, REQUIRED nonblank player-facing `description` (bounded to 1,024 characters), icon, Java handler, bounded parameters and enabled flag. Voting icons use the data description as gray nonitalic lore, with names in `#8C1708`; missing descriptions reject data validation. Timed descriptions say “Every so often” rather than exposing cadence. Production catalog is Sticky Keys, Inventory Shuffle, Owl Gene, Cursed Life, Statues, Gentle Breeze, Rocket Man, Time Glitch, Wet Noodle and Thin Skin. Tuned production intervals: Inventory Shuffle 360 ticks (18 seconds), Owl Gene 400 ticks (20 seconds), Statues 600 ticks (30 seconds). Owl/Statues retain 60 warning ticks and Statues retains 15 stillness ticks. Gentle Breeze acceleration is exactly halved from 0.008 to 0.004 per tick; its 0.15 speed cap is unchanged. Other mechanics retain existing timings/coverage/strengths. Selected definition snapshots keep their original parameters; tuning applies to newly offered/selected definitions after reload, not retroactively to active snapshots. `MadnessCombatContributor` is registered once in the canonical ordinary outgoing/incoming pipelines: Wet Noodle adds -0.15 to the shared outgoing bucket (neutral ordinary damage ×0.85; stacks additively with ordinary outgoing gear/enchantment bonuses), Thin Skin adds one ×1.15 incoming contribution. Live selected Trial state/current roster controls eligibility; no stale attributes, second damage events or fixed special interactions. True-channel, environmental and bypass-effect/execution sources are excluded. Clearing selected state/removing the participant immediately removes the contribution; both new definitions participate in the normal ballot/exclusion/snapshot lifecycle.

`MadnessState` persists pending counts, selected definition snapshots, shared offered definitions, votes, and a monotonic ballot serial (legacy saves default to zero). Threshold increments are differences in completed-count multiples of five. Normal DEAL/NO DEAL finishes before voting; cashed-out players are absent from the roster and vote tally. Initial Skip pending votes resolve before first actual room. Vanilla one-row chest menus are display-only. A successful submission durably records the vote, immediately closes the menu, invalidates that ballot's menu, and prevents automatic reopening or duplicate submissions. Unsubmitted players retain reopening behavior. Completion uses the current continuing roster, not original party size; roster removal is reconciled on the next Decision tick. All-voted resolves immediately; otherwise timeout retains plurality/top-only random ties/no-vote random selection. One guarded resolver re-reads the live session and checks ballot serial plus unconsumed options before publishing activation, announcing, closing menus and progressing. Stale last-vote/timeout work cannot resolve the ballot twice. Multiple pending selections resolve sequentially with cleared votes, fresh serial/options, and active-winner exclusions; later ballots retain their established independent 600-tick timeout. Exhausted pools consume pending selections without effects or blocking. Selected snapshots survive reload/room transitions; gameplay clocks pause outside physical active rooms. Server restart still aborts interrupted sessions and restores outside inventory. Participant-scoped hooks stop affecting departed/dead/disconnected players.

Prior voting hotfix remains intact: namespaced preset IDs use `IdentifierArgument`; successful votes durably submit then immediately close/invalidate menus, submitted players never auto-reopen, final current-roster vote resolves immediately, and roster removal reconciles on the next Decision tick. Exactly one named winner message in `#8C1708` is sent per active participant per resolved ballot. A live ballot serial/options guard prevents final-vote/timeout/stale-click duplicate activation/message/progression. Shared Decision countdown presents title/subtitle/basedrum before GUI branching and preserves positive remaining session ticks across Deal→Madness; sequential pending ballots retain their separate 600-tick timeouts and reset votes/options/serial. The final hotfix also corrects the namespaced debug Madness-ID parser and adds `madness` command suggestions.

Tuned production intervals: Inventory Shuffle 360 ticks (18 seconds), Owl Gene 400 ticks (20 seconds), Statues 600 ticks (30 seconds). Owl/Statues retain 60 warning ticks and Statues retains 15 stillness ticks. Gentle Breeze acceleration is exactly halved from 0.008 to 0.004 per tick; its 0.15 speed cap is unchanged. Selected definition snapshots keep their original parameters; tuning applies to newly offered/selected definitions after reload, not retroactively to active snapshots. `MadnessCombatContributor` is registered once in the canonical ordinary outgoing/incoming pipelines: Wet Noodle adds -0.15 to the shared outgoing bucket (neutral ordinary damage ×0.85; stacks additively with ordinary outgoing gear/enchantment bonuses), Thin Skin adds one ×1.15 incoming contribution. Live selected Trial state/current roster controls eligibility; no stale attributes, second damage events or fixed special interactions. True-channel, environmental and bypass-effect/execution sources are excluded. Clearing selected state/removing the participant immediately removes the contribution; both new definitions participate in the normal ballot/exclusion/snapshot lifecycle.

All twelve production room JSON definitions explicitly own handler/loadout references, base weight 5, enabled flag, native category, structures, spawn marker policy, and bounds. Live expanded pools come from loaded native categories, not the historical compatibility pool helper. `TrialRoomHandlerRegistry` registers bespoke start/loadout adapters and rejects duplicate registrations; unknown handler/loadout definitions are diagnosed and excluded from live selection. Existing room tick/control/wave/protection/party-scaling services remain bespoke Java. Stable room IDs remain the persistence/control identity: existing bespoke handlers are not a generic aliasable room-template system. Selection retains immediate previous-room exclusion and 5→3→1→ineligible actual-appearance decay; Snow Globe overlays cannot resurrect an exhausted room.

All four phase reward tables remain reloadable. ONLY four Madness Ballot rows were added (quantity exactly 1): Hardcore +1 weight 10 and +2 weight 3; Impossible +2 weight 8; Demonic +3 weight 8. Apprentice has none; Impossible has no +3. Prior rows, weights, quantities and duplicates are unchanged; no deferred Abandoned Spaceship row was enabled. Canonical catalog declarations and pinned totals now match these additions: Hardcore declared/active 122/117, Impossible 183/175, Demonic 143/139; Apprentice remains 89/89. Existing `static_item` materialization obtains typed registered trinket defaults. Fame Shop requires NO production changes: its aggregate Trial-catalog generator naturally materializes all three tiers, proven by seeded runtime catalogs. `trial_portal_preset` rewards retain concrete preset snapshot semantics.

Added/updated tests: `MadnessPolishTest` covers all ten required descriptions/hidden cadence, all three physical trinket application/consumption/copy/component-codec paths, stronger-replaces-weaker semantics, exact reward tier rows, native obfuscation/actual text across Decision and room states, and readability-change deduplication. `trial_madness_polish` exercises all ten real GUI icons/lore, typed inventory commands and application, ItemStack save/relog round-trip, actual 3/4/5 offered counts, timer wire codec, namespaced operator activation, outgoing/incoming math and composition, unchanged true packets, environmental/execution exclusions, selection and cleanup, scoped effect sound scheduler dispatch and exact pitches, and normal Fame Shop generation for all three tiers. Existing Madness/Decision/Skip-10/trinket/reward/damage/UI/persistence tests remain; old pinned reward-count assertions were updated solely for the new rows.

Verification: focused Madness/Trial Trinket/reward-data/combat/UI selection passes; full `gradlew.bat cleanTest test build` passes 706 tests / 173 suites with zero failures/errors/skips. All 22 loaded-server GameTests pass, including prior voting/countdown/Skip-10 hotfix and final polish. Server mock logins use NeoForge's `configureMockConnection`; no production network gating changed. Dedicated `runServer` loads 1,462 recipes, publishes content, validates recipe invariants and reaches `Done (0.607s)` without client classloading. Fresh `runClient` reloads `mod/cosmicpve`, initializes OpenAL/SoundEngine and item/GUI/other atlases with no new CosmicPVE resource/codec/sided-loading failure. Installed Slightly Improved Font pack has its unchanged unrelated metadata error. Startup-only server/client processes were stopped after verification; closed stdin required targeted process termination, so their run-task exit status is not a startup success gate. GameTestServer lacks `cosmic_instance`: Skip-10 tests cover 4/4/2 phase-tagged skipped reward positions and two early ballots at ordinal 11 Impossible, but substitute an empty room pool at final progression rather than claiming real placement/portal activation/reward rolls. Sound tests drive the real per-player scheduler/cue dispatch through connected mock players; no human playback acceptance. Candidate-owned whitespace check passes; global check only flags preserved user design whitespace.

Manual commands (permissioned `/cosmic trial` surface):

- Existing pending-ballot admin surface: `/cosmic trial debug continue` (runtime verified; requires an active post-room Decision Box with pending selections, no open ballot). There is no arbitrary modifier picker or instant timed-effect proc command. To get a fresh ballot normally, generate/place/activate the verified Skip-10 preset portal and finish the join window; selections remain random.
- Runtime-verified physical variants: `/cosmic trial trinket give @s madness 1`, `/cosmic trial trinket give @s madness 2`, `/cosmic trial trinket give @s madness 3`. Drag each onto a SINGLE Trial Portal; repeated same-family application is replacement only when stronger, not addition. With that portal in the main hand, `/cosmic trial portal inspect` is runtime verified and prints `madnessOptionBonus`; tooltip shows the bonus. Applying each fresh variant yields 3/4/5 options, capped by remaining unused definitions. Physical registered items already existed in the audited candidate; this hotfix corrects names/tooltip and adds reward availability rather than duplicating items.

Final presentation polish: names are `+1 Madness Ballot Trial Trinket`, `+2 Madness Ballot Trial Trinket`, `+3 Madness Ballot Trial Trinket`, bold `#8C1708`, using unchanged standard trinket vanilla-base models. Tooltips say “Adds 1 additional Madness Modifier option to each Madness ballot.” / “Adds 2 additional Madness Modifier options to each Madness ballot.” / “Adds 3 additional Madness Modifier options to each Madness ballot.” They modify only portal `madness_option_bonus`, never activate an effect.

Audio: `MadnessSounds` is a small common cue helper, NOT another timer. Rocket dispatches one participant-only `FIREWORK_ROCKET_BLAST` on launch. Statues dispatches participant-only `WITHER_SPAWN` at challenge start BEFORE the first note. Owl and Statues dispatch `NOTE_BLOCK_PLING` at 3/2/1 seconds with exact server/packet pitch arguments `0.0F`, `1.0F`, `2.0F`, volume 1.0; no duplicate 3-second cue on the next tick, and 2/1 cues land 20/40 ticks after start. Existing launch strength, fall damage, warning/window lengths and intervals are unchanged. Runtime packets retain pitch zero, but vanilla client `SoundEngine.calculatePitch` clamps audible playback to 0.5–2.0; the mod does not substitute 0.5 in its calls. Session/roster cleanup stops future cues.

Time Glitch: payload now contains real timer seconds plus a boolean `obfuscated` (matching updated client/server candidate required). Readability cadence remains unchanged. Shared `TrialTimerDisplayService.timerComponent` constructs normal actual timer text with `Style.withObfuscated`; the client caches/renders that Component directly, preserving native animated obfuscation, including Decision. Dedup tracks seconds AND hidden/readable transitions so a frozen Decision timer still flips styling. Hide uses -1 only; no -2/question-mark strategy remains. Authoritative Trial/Decision timers are untouched.

- Normal: `/cosmic trial portal give @s`.
- Presets: `/cosmic trial portal preset @s cosmicpve:skip_5`, `cosmicpve:skip_10`, `cosmicpve:fame_200`, `cosmicpve:madness_5_choices`, or `cosmicpve:combined` (replace the final ID in the full command).
- Runtime-verified exact Skip 10 command: `/cosmic trial portal preset @s cosmicpve:skip_10` (operator/GameMaster permissions; the test executes this registered tree with an online mock player and verifies actual inventory delivery).
- Ballots: `/cosmic trial trinket give @s madness 1` (or 2/3); drag onto a single portal.
- Inspect: `/cosmic trial portal inspect`, `/cosmic trial debug status`, `/cosmic trial debug complete-room`; complete rooms 1–5 then NO DEAL to exercise the ordinary threshold. Skip 10 requires two full votes before physical ordinal 11; inspect phase-tagged 4/4/2 rewards, frozen 16,800-tick timer, pending count, and untouched actual weights before the first room.
- Runtime-verified effect commands (active Trial required, finish open ballot first): `/cosmic trial debug madness add cosmicpve:wet_noodle`, `/cosmic trial debug madness add cosmicpve:thin_skin`, `/cosmic trial debug madness add cosmicpve:rocket_man`, `/cosmic trial debug madness add cosmicpve:statues`, `/cosmic trial debug madness add cosmicpve:owl_gene`, `/cosmic trial debug madness add cosmicpve:time_glitch`. Damage modifiers apply immediately to selected active participants; timed effects run only in physical ROOM_ACTIVE. No instant-proc command was added: Rocket retains 500 ticks, Owl 400 + 60 warning ticks, Statues 600 + 60 warning ticks + 15 stillness ticks. These cadences are operator documentation, not voting lore.

Healing-path audit: gameplay enchantment/item/potion/regeneration heals use the normal heal event. `/heal` and `/restore` were the one ordinary restoration bypass discovered; living-player restoration now uses that same event, so Cursed Life applies there too. Explicit operator health assignment and the existing dead-player reset are not ordinary healing.

Still requiring manual acceptance: human-visible ten-option lore, Wet Noodle/Thin Skin real ordinary combat with gear and cleanup, actual trinket drag/drop/relog, audible sound order/vanilla playback limits, animated Minecraft obfuscation glyphs in physical rooms and Decision, real DEAL/disconnect/payout flows, a fresh placed Skip-10 portal with rolled rewards and physical Impossible room 11 after two ballots, and all ten effects across a real run. The hotfix deliberately does not fix other modifier-specific manual findings. No overall manual acceptance and no next Roadmap work.

Startup verification server/client processes have been stopped.

## Maintaining This Handoff

`DEV_HANDOFF.md` is a living current-state document, not a historical changelog.

After **every** successfully verified major milestone:

- update the milestone ledger;
- update current implemented systems and content;
- incorporate newly settled canonical semantics;
- move resolved items out of Unresolved;
- add newly discovered unresolved issues;
- update the Current Next Milestone;
- update the verified Git commit after the milestone has been committed;
- remove obsolete implementation descriptions rather than preserving contradictory historical versions.

Do not let this file grow indefinitely. Keep it concise, consolidate completed history, and never record speculative ideas as implemented facts. When a Codex implementation prompt includes “Update DEV_HANDOFF.md,” the update is part of required milestone completion, not optional cleanup.
